# Ireland asset coverage crosswalk

Generated 2026-08-03 by `docs/plans/ireland_focus_tree/assets/build_asset_coverage_crosswalk.py`.

This is the row-level handoff between canonical requirements, source or generated evidence, Ireland-owned runtime registrations, and live implementation consumers. `assets/` is staging only; every runtime path in this report is rooted under the mod's standard `gfx/` tree. A row marked blocked or needs_user_review is deliberately not cleared by registration.

## Coverage summary

| surface | canonical rows | runtime registrations | consumer surface | visual/source status |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| Focus goal icons | 216 | 216 | `common/national_focus/slopx_ireland_focus_tree.txt` + paired shine masks | 216 independently generated emblems accepted after complete-sheet HOI4-style review; runtime DDS promoted under gfx |
| Decision categories | 13 | 13 | `common/decisions/categories/slopx_ireland_categories.txt` | registered |
| Named decision actions | 140 | 140 unique action sprites | `common/decisions/slopx_ireland_decisions.txt` | 70 controlled-reuse textures |
| Missions | 54 | 54 | `common/decisions/slopx_ireland_decisions.txt` | registered; mission outcome blocks remain code-owned |
| Persistent route spirits | 63 | 93 | `common/ideas/slopx_ireland_spirits.txt` | three families × 21 staged route entries |
| Temporary overlays | 10 durable overlays + 17 event/temporary overlays | 27 | decision/event/controller consumers | registered; conditional state bindings audited per row |
| Law icons | 51 | 51 | scripted law stages in `common/scripted_effects/slopx_ireland_laws.txt` | registered; dynamic stage-to-sprite binding is recorded per row |
| Mechanic/ledger icons | 45 | 45 | BOP and scripted GUI surfaces | registered; direct consumer evidence listed per row |
| Achievement states | 96 | 96 | `common/achievements/slopx_ireland_achievements.txt` | 32 × completed/grey/not-eligible |
| Route/symbol assets | 27 | 30 | route character and route-state consumers | generated source packages; runtime DDS present |
| Scripted GUI assets | 11 | 11 | three GUI layouts | static runtime package; state surface listed per row |
| Independent animations | 7 packages / 14 registrations | 14 | three GUI surfaces and event/evidence states | frame sheets + static fallbacks |
| Generated event/news art | 51 | 51 | event/news code consumers | generated source/processed package; runtime DDS present |
| Source-package registrations | 169 manifest rows | 170 registered rows | leaders, symbols, reports | 3 blocked/review rows remain unpromoted |

The canonical goal/law visual evidence is the fresh regenerated package at `assets/ireland_focus_tree/icons/regenerated_goal_and_law_icons_fresh_2026-08-01_v1/`. It contains 216 fresh 100x88 focus-emblem sources and 51 fresh 60x68 law-emblem sources, with per-row source, alpha, processed, DDS, runtime, and readback records. Source and processing evidence remain in staging; final DDS files belong under the mod's standard `gfx/` tree.

## Audit interpretation

- Earlier focus-icon normalization and v5-v12 packages are retained as technical or rejected evidence only. The fresh 2026-08-01 package is the accepted goal/law source of truth after complete-sheet boundary, chroma, alignment, and runtime readback review.
- The 216 focus and 51 law rows are independently regenerated source art; the runtime names and `.gfx` registrations remain stable while the verified DDS files are promoted under `gfx/interface/goals/slopx_ireland/` and `gfx/interface/ideas/slopx_ireland/laws/`.
- Decision-action rows distinguish 140 named consumers from the 70 allocated DDS textures. Reuse is recorded at the row level and is not a free placeholder: each reused texture has a named decision consumer and category binding.
- The law, overlay, and mechanic packages are registered assets whose dynamic state controllers are intentionally listed in the consumer column. A missing direct `GFX_` token is an audit finding, not a claim that the asset is used.
- Portrait, symbol/document, and source event/news clearance remains governed by the five-state manifest. The manifest contains 60 portrait rows, 40 symbol/document rows, and 69 source event/news rows; 3 blocked/review rows remain unpromoted and are not cleared by this crosswalk.
- Live HOI4 engine, GUI click-region, frame playback, texture-loading, and scenario validation remain intentionally unrun and are excluded from the completion gate by user direction; this report is static coverage evidence.
